Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on hyperbolic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 861. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 446 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_861(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 861. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^446(S, F) is trivial. QED.
